local cm=(function(SD,Vu)
    SD=MC(SD)
    local Sc=Sy()
    local function ep(Es,_d)
        local Rp=(function(...)
            return{...},WD('#',...)
        end)
        local ah;
        ah=(function(Vi,pa,do_)
            if pa>do_ then
                return
            end
            return Vi[pa],ah(Vi,pa+1,do_)
        end)
        local function KF(jF,vD,Hc,KC)
            local zr,HG,Em,Bi,Ya,Jr,wh_,sF,Yz,sn,Tk,Sq,Gp,tc,ME,vi,re_,zH,OG,_n,oE,hu,db,rj;

                Ya[rD]=Oc(_D,58696)-Oc(Vc,1137)

            end;

            while Gp~=25769 do
